Need robust code. Let's outline final program:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _nextpow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _conv_fft(a, nfft=None):
   if nfft is None: nfft = _nextpow2(2*len(a)-1)
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*len(a)-1]

def _active_grad(q, a, K, delta, use_fft_if_many=...):
   n=len(a); mlen=len(q)
   # select candidates near max
   # to avoid sorting all, use argpartition
   if K >= mlen:
      w = q / q.sum()? return fft grad
   idx = np.argpartition(-q, min(K, mlen-1))[-K:] if K<mlen else np.arange(mlen)
   # but if q has ties near max, top K ok.
   vals = q[idx]
   maxv=vals.max()
   # weights exp((vals-maxv)/delta)
   # delta maybe = max(eps, maxv*1e-4)
   # if weights too small, normalize
   w = np.exp((vals - maxv)/delta)
   s=w.sum(); w/=s
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # optionally if K > 80, compute FFT gradient
   if K > 128:
      # create lam full
      lam=np.zeros(mlen); lam[idx]=w
      # fft crosscorr
   else:
      for k, weight in zip(idx, w):
          if weight < 1e-10: continue
          lo = max(0, k - (n-1))
          hi = min(n-1, k) + 1
          # idx segment: start = k - (hi-1), stop = k - lo + 1
          grad[lo:hi] += weight * a[k-(hi-1):k-lo+1][::-1]
      grad *= 2.0
   return grad
```

Pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kw):
 t0=time.monotonic()
 deadline = t0 + budget_s - 30  # headroom, maybe 60
 rng = default_rng(seed)
 # load base
 base = height_sequence_1 if available else ones fallback
 n = len(base)
 S = float(n)
 # maybe cap base and normalize
 def normalize_a(a): clip, sum -> S
 def fast_conv(a): fft
 def score_fast(a, q=None): q=... ; return 2*n*q.max()/S*S
 def active_grad(q,a,K, delta): ...

Softmax:
```
def y_to_a(y):
   y = np.clip(y, -50.0, y_upper)
   mz = y.max()
   ex = np.exp(y - mz)
   s = ex.sum()
   if s <=0 or not finite: ex = np.ones(n); s=n
   p = ex/s
   cap = p_cap
   if p.max() > cap:
       over = np.maximum(p - cap, 0)
       p = np.minimum(p, cap)
       excess = over.sum()
       if excess > 0:
           mask = p < cap
           cnt = mask.sum()
           if cnt>0:
               p[mask] += excess/cnt
               p -= (p.sum()-1)/n? Actually renormalize.
           else: p.fill(1/n)
       p /= p.sum()
   a = S*p
   return a
```

Pseudo optimize_y(y_init, seconds, lr0, K0, K1, wtemp0, ...):
```
 y = y_init.copy()
 a, q, M, score = y_to_a_score(y)
 best_y, best_score = y.copy(), score
 m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); adam_step=0
 lr=lr0
 lr_decay = 0.98** (1/1000)? compute per step
 no_improve=0
 failed_streak=0
 while time < end:
   # schedule
   frac = min(1, iterations/total?) unknown. Use step fraction based on elapsed / seconds.
   K = int(round(K0 + (K1-K0)*frac)) maybe ensure >=1
   wtemp = max(1e-12, M*(wtemp0 + (wtemp1-wtemp0)*frac))
   grad_a = active_grad(q,a,K,wtemp)
   # maybe center? natural gradient already centers via p weighted; but we can subtract mean(p*grad) weighted. Formula exactly.
   mean_g = float(p @ grad_a) # p = a/S
   grad_y = S * p * (grad_a - mean_g)
   # optional clip grad_y norm
   # update adam
   adam_step +=1
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad_y
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad_y*grad_y
   mb = m/(1-b1**adam_step)
   vb = v/(1-b2**adam_step)
   direction = -(mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+eps))
   # maybe normalize direction norm for stable line search: dir_norm = 1/sqrt(direction@direction) but can be O(1). Step size alpha.
   alpha = lr
   # line search
   accepted=False
   # To avoid too many trials if time low: max_trials=4
   for trial in range(5):
       if time > deadline: break
       y_trial = y + alpha * direction
       y_trial = np.clip(y_trial, y_lower, y_upper)
       score_trial, q_trial, M_trial, a_trial = eval_y_trial(y_trial)
       if score_trial < score - 1e-15: # strict
           accept
           # maybe if trial not first, adapt lr new = alpha*1.1
           break
       alpha *= 0.5
   if accepted:
       y, q, M, score, a = ...
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: best=..., no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       lr = min(lr_max, alpha*1.05?) maybe maintain
       if no_improve > patience: break? or restart with perturbed y
   else:
       failed_streak +=1
       lr *= 0.5
       # update ema? we already did with gradient but rejected. Maybe okay.
       if failed_streak > 20: break
```

Cap correction algorithm:
```
def finalize_p(p):
  c = p_cap
  if p.max() > c:
     # Project p onto box [0,c] and sum 1 (Euclidean-ish)
     # Simple iterative: set p=min(p,c); distribute excess to entries with room.
     for _ in range(3):
        over = p.max()-c
        if over <= 1e-15: break
        p = np.minimum(p,c)
        excess = 1 - p.sum()
        mask = p < c
        if mask.sum()==0: p.fill(1/n); break
        p[mask] += excess / mask.sum()
  s=p.sum(); if not finite: p=ones/n; else p/=s
  return p
```
Since p_cap*n=1000, always room. Good. This loops at most few.

y = np.clip(y, y_lower, y_upper)
ym = y.max()
# if not finite, reset
ex = np.exp(y-ym)
s=ex.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: ex=ones; s=n

if p.max() > p_cap:
    # projection
    p = np.minimum(p, p_cap)
    # loop up to 4
    for _ in range(4):
       excess = 1.0 - p.sum()
       if abs(excess) < 1e-14: break
       mask = p < p_cap
       if not mask.any(): p.fill(1.0/n); break

```
def direct_grad(q,a,K,wtemp):
   m=len(q)
   if K>=m: idx=np.arange(m)
   else:
      # argpartition; if K==1, use max? To include near max maybe K=1.
      idx = np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]  # if K=m? okay
   vals = q[idx]
   maxv = vals.max()
   # if wtemp <=0 or maxv==0: uniform among top K?
   if wtemp <= 0 or not finite:
       w = np.full(K, 1.0/K)
   else:
       z = (vals - maxv) / wtemp
       z = np.maximum(z, -700.0)
       w = np.exp(z)
       sw = w.sum()
       if sw <=0 or not finite: w = np.full(K,1.0/K)
       else: w /= sw
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # Use precomputed arrays
   # skip weights too small
   for t in range(K):
      lk = int(idx[t]); wt = w[t]
      if wt < 1e-10: continue
      lo = lo_arr[lk]; hi = hi_arr[lk]; st = start_arr[lk]; sp = stop_arr[lk]
      grad[lo:hi] += wt * a[st:sp][::-1]
   return 2.0*grad
```
If K small and argpartition returns unsorted, weights okay. If K=1, w=1.

Precompute arrays as Python lists? numpy int arrays. Slicing with numpy scalars okay. To reduce overhead, convert to Python int lists? `lo_list = lo_arr.tolist()`, etc. Then indexing gives Python ints. Use lists maybe faster. m=13923; lists memory okay. Precompute `lo_l`, `hi_l`, `st_l`, `sp_l`.

Pseudo optimize:
```
def optimize_y(y, end_time, lr0=0.05, K0=64, K1=8, wtemp_rel0=0.01, wtemp_rel1=0.001, reset_patience=1000, max_restarts=2):
    y = np.clip(y.copy(), y_lower, y_upper)
    a = y_to_a(y)
    q = fast_conv(a); M=float(q.max()); score=score_from_M(M)
    best_y = y.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_M=M; best_q=q.copy()
    m_adam=np.zeros(n); v_adam=np.zeros(n); step=0; lr=lr0; no_improve=0; fails=0; restarts=0; last_exact=0; exact_best_score_holder? Use nonlocal.
    # exact eval initial? maybe already
    # maybe if score exact? no
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       # schedule fraction based on step/5000 or elapsed? Use min(1, step/3000)
       frac = min(1.0, step/3000.0)
       K = int(max(1, round(K0 + (K1-K0)*frac)))
       wtemp = max(1e-12, M * (wtemp_rel0 + (wtemp_rel1-wtemp_rel0)*frac))
       grad_a = direct_grad(q,a,K,wtemp)
       # if gradient zero, break
       p = a/S
       mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad_a))
       grad_y = S * p * (grad_a - mean_g)
       if not finite or norm 0: perturb? break
       step +=1
       m_adam = b1*m_adam + (1-b1)*grad_y
       v_adam = b2*v_adam + (1-b2)*(grad_y*grad_y)
       mb = m_adam/(1-b1**step)
       vb = v_adam/(1-b2**step)
       direction = -(mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8))
       # normalize direction to RMS 1 maybe
       rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction))
       if rms>0: direction /= rms
       alpha = lr
       accepted=False
       # Maybe if step small, skip line search? no
       for _ in range(4):
           if time.monotonic() >= end_time: break
           y_trial = y + alpha*direction
           y_trial = np.clip(y_trial, y_lower, y_upper)
           a_trial = y_to_a(y_trial)
           q_trial = fast_conv(a_trial); M_trial=float(q_trial.max())
           score_trial = 2.0*n*M_trial/(S*S)
           if score_trial < score - 1e-15:
               y = y_trial; a=a_trial; q=q_trial; M=M_trial; score=score_trial
               accepted=True
               lr = min(0.2, alpha*1.1)  # or if alpha > lr? update to alpha*1.05
               break
           alpha *= 0.5
           if alpha < 1e-8: break
       if accepted:
          if score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=score; best_score; best_y=y.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
             # exact eval condition
             if exact_best_holder is not None and (score < exact_holder[0] - 1e-5 or score < best_holder[0]-1e-5) and now-last_exact>15 and exact_count<max:
                exc=exact_score(best_a)
                if finite: update holder; exact_count++
                last_exact=now
          else:
             no_improve +=1
       else:
          fails +=1
          lr *= 0.5
          no_improve +=1
          if fails > 100:
             # restart perturb from best_y
             restarts +=1
             if restarts > max_restarts: break
             noise = 0.1 / restarts
             y = np.clip(best_y + noise*rng.standard_normal(n), y_lower, y_upper)
             a = y_to_a(y); q=fast_conv(a); M=...; score=...
             m_adam=v_adam=0; step=0; lr=lr0; no_improve=fails=0
             continue
       if no_improve > 2000:
          # restart
          restarts +=1
          if restarts > max_restarts: break
          noise = 0.08/restarts
          y = np.clip(best_y + noise*rng.standard_normal(n), y_lower, y_upper)
          a=...; reset
    return best_y,best_a,best_score
```

We can analytically consider symmetric densities. Because convolution support [0,2], to minimize max, one might want symmetric p to balance edges. For symmetric p, q is symmetric; maximum possibly near center or edges depending singularities. One-sided 1/√x gave max at t in [0,1] constant π/4, score π/2=1.5708. Grad computation with full direct/FFT:
```
def smooth_grad_from_z(q,a,beta):
   M=float(q.max())
   z=beta*(q-M)
   # avoid underflow overflow
   z=np.clip(z, -740.0, 0.0)
   wu=np.exp(z)
   wsum=wu.sum()
   if not finite or wsum<=0: weights uniform over max indices? F=M
   F=M+math.log(wsum)/beta
   # threshold for direct: wu > max(1e-12*wsum, 1e-300)
   sig = wu > (1e-8 * wsum)
   cnt=sig.sum()
   if cnt ==0: ctx? uniform over argmax
   if cnt <= max_direct:
       idx = np.nonzero(sig)[0]
       w = wu[idx]/wsum
       grad = direct with w
   else:
       # use FFT full
       lam = wu / wsum
       grad = fft_corr(lam,a)*2
   return F, grad
```

def compute_F_grad(q,a,beta, need_grad=True):
   M = float(q.max())
   z = beta*(q-M)
   z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
   wu = np.exp(z)
   wsum = float(wu.sum())
   if wsum <=0 or not finite: F=M; w=None
   else: F=M+math.log(wsum)/beta; w = wu/wsum if need full? For direct, use wu thresholds
```

```
def obj_F(q, a, beta):
    M = float(q.max())
    if beta <= 1e-12: return M, M
    z = beta*(q-M)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    wsum = float(np.exp(z).sum())
    if not finite or wsum<=0: F=M
    else: F = M + math.log(wsum)/beta
    return F, M
```

def fast_conv(a):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, nfftC)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfftC)
    # maybe out has length nfftC; if negative tiny, no
    return out[:m]
```

`F_and_M(q,beta)`:
```
def F_and_M(q,beta):
   M=float(q.max())
   if beta <= 1e-8: return M,M
   z=beta*(q-M)
   # avoid overflow: q-M <=0, but due roundoff maybe positive tiny. Clip max to 0.
   z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
   wsum=float(np.exp(z).sum())
   if not finite or wsum<=0: return M,M
   return M + math.log(wsum)/beta, M
```
Creating z,w each trial. Could optimize by `z = q - M`; multiply. Fine.

def grad_F(q,a,beta):
   M=float(q.max())
   if beta<=1e-8:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))]); grad=2*direct_raw(idx, np.array([1.0])? )
      return M,M,grad? F=M
   z=beta*(q-M); z=np.clip(z,-700,0); wu=np.exp(z); wsum=float(wu.sum())
   if not finite or wsum<=0: F=M; ...
   else: F=M+math.log(wsum)/beta
   threshold=1e-8*wsum
   idx=np.flatnonzero(wu > threshold)
   if idx.size ==0:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))]); w=np.array([1.0]);
   elif idx.size <= max_direct:
      w = wu[idx]/wsum
      # if sum w <1? add mass? It's fine.
      grad = direct_weights(idx, w)
   else:
      lam = wu/wsum
      grad = fft_grad(lam)
   return F,M,grad
```

def direct_weights(idx, w):
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # if idx numpy; w numpy
   for i,k in enumerate(idx):
      wk=float(w[i])
      if wk <=1e-12: continue
      lk=int(k)
      lo=lo_l[lk]; hi=hi_l[lk]; st=st_l[lk]; sp=sp_l[lk]
      grad[lo:hi] += wk * a[st:sp][::-1]
   return grad*2.0
```

```
def fft_grad(lam):
   la = lam[::-1]
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, sizeG)
   fl = np.fft.rfft(la, sizeG)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fl, sizeG)
   seg = conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   return 2.0 * seg[::-1]
```

Pseudo full code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    headroom=max(5.0,min(60.0,0.1*total))
    hard_deadline=t0+total-headroom
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

    def time_ok(): return time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0

    # base load
    try:
       base=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       base=np.ones(6962,dtype=np.float64)
    if base.size==0:
       base=np.ones(6962,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(base)):
       base=np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    if base.size==0 or base.sum()<=1e-12:
       base=np.ones(base.size if base.size else 6962,dtype=np.float64)
    base=np.clip(base,0.0,1000.0)
    n=int(base.size)
    if n<=0: return [1.0]
    S=float(n)
    S2=S*S
    m=2*n-1
    nfftC=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    g_len=max(1,3*n-2)
    sizeG=1 << (g_len-1).bit_length()
    ks=np.arange(m,dtype=np.int64)
    lo_arr=np.maximum(0, ks-(n-1))
    hi_arr=np.minimum(n-1, ks)+1
    start_arr=ks-(hi_arr-1)
    stop_arr=ks-lo_arr+1
    lo_l=lo_arr.tolist(); hi_l=hi_arr.tolist(); st_l=start_arr.tolist(); sp_l=stop_arr.tolist()

    p_cap = min(1.0 - 1e-12, max(1.0/n + 1e-12, 1000.0/S if S>0 else 1.0))
    y_lower=-35.0
    y_upper=math.log(max(p_cap,1e-300))
    if y_upper < y_lower: y_upper=max(y_lower, math.log(p_cap))
    LOG_EPS=1e-12

    def normalize_a(a):
        a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            if a.size==0: a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
            else: a=np.resize(a,n)
        a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
        s=float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
            return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
        a=a*(S/s)
        a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
        s2=float(a.sum())
        if s2 <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(s2):
            return np.full(n, S/n)
        return a*(S/s2)

    def make_y(a):
        a=normalize_a(a)
        y=np.log(np.maximum(a, LOG_EPS))
        return np.clip(y, y_lower, y_upper)

    def y_to_a(y):
        y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
        y=np.clip(y, y_lower, y_upper)
        ym=float(y.max())
        if not math.isfinite(ym):
            return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
        ex=np.exp(y-ym)
        s=float(ex.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            ex=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64); s=float(n)
        p=ex/s
        # cap p max
        if p.max() > p_cap:
            p=np.minimum(p,p_cap)
            for _ in range(4):
                ps=float(p.sum())
                if not math.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0:
                   p=np.ones(n)/n; break
                excess=1.0-ps
                if abs(excess) < 1e-14:
                   break
                mask = p < p_cap
                cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
                if cnt == 0:
                   p.fill(1.0/n); break
                p[mask] += excess / cnt
            p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
            ps=float(p.sum())
            if ps > 0 and math.isfinite(ps): p=p/ps
            else: p=np.ones(n)/n
        return S*p

    def fast_conv(a):
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, nfftC)
        out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfftC)
        return out[:m]

    def score_M_from_M(M): return 2.0*n*M/S2
    def score_F_from_F(F): return 2.0*n*F/S2

    max_direct=100
    def direct_grad(widx, wgt, a):
        grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
        # If widx maybe numpy int array; wgt floats
        for k, wt in zip(widx, wgt):
            wt=float(wt)
            if wt <= 1e-14: continue
            k=int(k)
            lo=lo_l[k]; hi=hi_l[k]; st=st_l[k]; sp=sp_l[k]
            grad[lo:hi] += wt * a[st:sp][::-1]
        grad *= 2.0
        return grad

    def fft_grad(lam, a):
        if len(lam) == 0: return np.zeros(n)
        rl=lam[::-1]
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, sizeG)
        fl=np.fft.rfft(rl, sizeG)
        conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fl, sizeG)
        seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
        return 2.0 * seg[::-1]

    def F_and_M(q, beta):
        M=float(q.max())
        if beta <= 1e-8 or not math.isfinite(M):
            return M, M
        z=beta*(q-M)
        z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        wsum=float(np.exp(z).sum())
        if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            return M, M
        return M + math.log(wsum)/beta, M

    def grad_F(q, a, beta):
        M=float(q.max())
        if beta <= 1e-8 or not math.isfinite(M):
            idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w=np.array([1.0])
            grad=direct_grad(idx,w,a)
            return M,M,grad
        z=beta*(q-M)
        z=np.clip(z,-700.0,0.0)
        wu=np.exp(z)
        wsum=float(wu.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            F=M
            idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w=np.array([1.0])
            return F,M,direct_grad(idx,w,a)
        F=M + math.log(wsum)/beta
        threshold=1e-8*wsum
        widx=np.flatnonzero(wu > threshold)
        if widx.size == 0:
            widx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w=np.array([1.0])
        elif widx.size <= max_direct:
            w=wu[widx]/wsum
            # If sum is too small due threshold, rescale
            sw=float(w.sum())
            if sw <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(sw):
                widx=np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
                w=np.array([1.0])
            else:
                w=w/sw  # This renormalizes selected weights; F gradient changes if omitted mass not negligible. Better not renormalize if omitted small. But if sum close1, negligible. If sum <0.999 due threshold, renormalizing adds small mass; okay. Could keep w as is. To be exact, keep w (sum <=1). Gradient of F missing omitted mass. If omitted 1e-8, fine. Renormalizing makes scale 1, but omitted mass becomes zero instead of 1e-8; also fine. Use renormalize to maintain scale.
        else:
            lam=wu/wsum
            return F,M,fft_grad(lam,a)
        return F,M,direct_grad(widx,w,a)

    # exact evaluation
    state = {
       'best_m_score': math.inf,
       'best_m_a': None,
       'best_m_y': None,
       'best_exact_score': math.inf,
       'best_exact_a': None,
       'last_exact': 0.0,
       'exact_count': 0
    }
    def exact_score(a):
       try:
          out=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
          s=evaluate_sequence(out)
          return float(s) if math.isfinite(s) else math.inf
       except Exception:
          return math.inf
    def consider_candidate(a, do_exact=False):
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum()
       if s <= 0.0: return
       a=(a*s? Need normalize to S to match)?? For score invariant, we can leave? But p_cap? Use normalize_a(a) for surrogate. For exact, scaling doesn't change unless cap. If a has max>1000 after normalize? use normalize_a.
       an=normalize_a(a)
       q=fast_conv(an); M=float(q.max()); sc=score_M_from_M(M)
       if sc < state['best_m_score']:
          state['best_m_score']=sc; state['best_m_a']=an.copy(); state['best_m_y']=make_y(an)
       if do_exact and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20:
          ex=exact_score(an)
          if ex < state['best_exact_score']:
             state['best_exact_score']=ex; state['best_exact_a']=an.copy()
          state['exact_count'] += 1
          state['last_exact']=time.monotonic()
```
Need be careful in `consider_candidate` if do_exact False but exact state perhaps not set. At start, call with do_exact True for base. But exact_count limit? Add if state['exact_count'] >= 30 no exact. Also if now-last_exact < 10 and count>5 skip.

Let's write robust normalize:
```
def normalize_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n: ...
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not finite or s <= 1e-15: return np.full(n, S/n)
    a=a*(S/s)
    # enforce cap by iterative redistribution
    for _ in range(10):
        maxv=float(a.max())
        if maxv <= 1000.0 + 1e-9:
            break
        a=np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
        cur=float(a.sum())
        excess=S - cur
        if excess <= 1e-12:
            # sum still too high? scale down slightly all?
            a *= (S/cur if cur>0 else 1.0)
        else:
            mask = a < 1000.0
            cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                # S > 1000n, infeasible under cap; fill 1000 and accept sum maybe?
                a.fill(1000.0)
                break
            a[mask] += excess / cnt
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S/n)
    if abs(s - S) > 1e-9 * max(1.0, S):
        a = a * (S / s)
        a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    # final correction if sum not S due cap? If too low, add to below cap
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < S:
        excess=S-s
        mask=a<1000
        if mask.any(): a[mask]+=excess/mask.sum()
        else: a.fill(S/n)
    elif s > S + 1e-9*S:
        a *= S/s
        a=np.minimum(a,1000)
    return a
```
This is okay. But if we rescale at end after cap, may reexceed; one pass enough for typical. We can simplify for code length. Since cap rarely binding, maybe simple:
```
a=a*(S/s); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s2=a.sum(); if s2>0: a=a*(S/s2); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
```
If this leaves sum not S due cap, exact evaluator will clip and score different. But rare. Use loop robust but concise.

Maybe use a helper `project_to_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(a, S, cap=1000):
   a=np.clip(a,0,cap)
   for _ in range(8):
      s=a.sum()
      if s<=0: return np.full(n,S/n)
      a*=S/s
      over=a>cap
      if not over.any(): return a
      a[over]=cap
   # fallback distribute
   s=a.sum(); excess=S-s; mask=a<cap; if mask.any(): a[mask]+=excess/mask.sum(); return np.clip(a,0,cap)
```
This maintains sum approximately. Use.

```
def project_p(p, cap):
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n
   p/=s
   cap_eff = min(cap, 1.0-1e-15)
   for _ in range(10):
      if p.max() <= cap_eff + 1e-14: break
      p=np.minimum(p, cap_eff)
      s=p.sum()
      if s <=0: p=ones/n; break
      p/=s
   # if p.max() maybe still due all cap? add
   diff=1-p.sum()
   if diff>1e-12:
      mask=p<cap_eff
      if mask.any(): p[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
   elif diff < -1e-12:
      # scale down
      p*=1/p.sum()
   return p
```
This works if cap_eff*n>=1. cap=1000/S; for n=6962 cap=0.143, product=1000. Good. For n=1, cap=1000? min cap 1.0? p_cap=min(1-eps, max(1/n+eps,1000/S)) =1-eps, product ~1. Good.

def optimize(y0, end_time, lr0, beta0, beta1, max_restarts, hard_stop=True):
   start_tmp=time.monotonic()
   dur=max(1.0, end_time-start_tmp)
   y=np.clip(y0.copy(), y_lower, y_upper).astype(float)
   a=y_to_a(y)
   q=fast_conv(a)
   beta=beta0
   F,M,grad = grad_F(q,a,beta)
   score_F=score_F_from_F(F); score_M=score_M_from_M(M)
   best_y=y.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_score_M=score_M
   # update state best_m? maybe use current. We can call consider? Not exact.
   if score_M < state['best_m_score']: state['best_m_score']=score_M; state['best_m_a']=a.copy(); state['best_m_y']=y.copy()
   m_adam=np.zeros(n); v_adam=np.zeros(n); step=0
   lr=lr0; no_improve=0; fails=0; restarts=0
   # if current score improves exact? maybe not
   while time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
      now=time.monotonic()
      frac=min(1.0,(now-start_tmp)/(0.85*dur))
      beta=beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*frac
      # maybe if no_improve high, beta = min(200, beta*1.2)
      # compute grad
      F,M,grad = grad_F(q,a,beta)
      score_F=score_F_from_F(F); score_M=score_M_from_M(M)
      # update best_m if M lower
      if score_M < state['best_m_score'] - 1e-14:
         state['best_m_score']=score_M; state['best_m_a']=a.copy(); state['best_m_y']=y.copy()
      p=a/S
      mean_g=float(np.dot(p, grad))
      gy=S * p * (grad - mean_g)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)) or float(np.dot(gy,gy)) <= 1e-30:
          # perturb or break
          break
      step+=1
      b1=0.9; b2=0.999
      m_adam = b1*m_adam + (1-b1)*gy
      v_adam = b2*v_adam + (1-b2)*(gy*gy)
      mb = m_adam/(1-b1**step)
      vb = v_adam/(1-b2**step)
      direction = -(mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8))
      rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
      if not math.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
          direction = -gy
          rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
      if not math.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
          break
      direction = direction / rms
      alpha=lr
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(5):
         if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 1.0: break

         if not np.all(np.isfinite(y_trial)): accepted=False; break? alpha halve
         y_trial=np.clip(y_trial, y_lower, y_upper)
         a_trial=y_to_a(y_trial)
         q_trial=fast_conv(a_trial)
         F_trial,M_trial = F_and_M(q_trial,beta)
         sF_trial=score_F_from_F(F_trial)
         if sF_trial < score_F - 1e-13:
            # accept
            y=y_trial; a=a_trial; q=q_trial; F=F_trial; M=M_trial; score_F=sF_trial; score_M=score_M_from_M(M_trial)
            accepted=True
            # adapt lr
            if alpha > lr:
                lr=min(0.5, alpha)
            else:
                lr=min(0.5, alpha*1.1)
            break
         alpha *= 0.5
         if alpha < 1e-8: break
      if accepted:
         if score_M < state['best_m_score'] - 1e-12:
             state['best_m_score']=score_M; state['best_m_a']=a.copy(); state['best_m_y']=y.copy()
             no_improve=0
         else:

             state['last_exact']=time.monotonic(); state['exact_count']+=1

         fails += 1
         no_improve += 1
         lr = max(1e-5, lr*0.5)
         if fails > 50:
             restarts += 1
             if restarts > max_restarts:
                 break
             scale=0.12/(restarts+0.5)

Let's refine project_p and normalize_a code to avoid bugs.

```
def project_p(p, cap):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size != n:
       p=np.ones(n)/n if p.size==0 else np.resize(p,n)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): p=np.ones(n)/n
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s): return np.ones(n)/n
    p = p/s
    cap = min(float(cap), 1.0)
    if cap >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
       return p
    # If cap*n < 1 due roundoff? set cap=1
    if cap * n < 1.0:
       return p  # no feasible strict cap; return normalized
    for _ in range(12):
       mx=float(p.max())
       if mx <= cap + 1e-13:
          break
       p = np.minimum(p, cap)
       s=float(p.sum())
       if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s):
          p=np.ones(n)/n; break
       p = p/s
    # final sum correction
    s=float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       p=np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       p = p/s
       diff=1.0 - float(p.sum())
       if diff > 1e-12:
           mask = p < cap
           cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
           if cnt:
              p[mask] += diff/cnt
              p = p / float(p.sum())
           else:
              p.fill(1.0/n)
       elif diff < -1e-12:
           p = p / float(p.sum())
    return np.clip(p,0.0,1.0)
```
If p has max cap and after p/s max may slightly exceed due float; loop. Good.

```
def normalize_a(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size != n: ...
   a=np.maximum(0.0,a)
   if not finite: replace
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return full S/n
   p=a/s
   p=project_p(p, p_cap)
   return S*p
```

Project_p:
```
    def project_p(p, cap):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n if p.size == 0 else np.resize(p, n)
        p = np.maximum(0.0, p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        p = p / s
        cap = float(cap)
        if cap >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
            return p
        if cap * n < 1.0 - 1e-12:
            # infeasible; fall back uniform
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        for _ in range(12):
            mx = float(p.max())
            if mx <= cap + 1e-13:
                break
            p = np.minimum(p, cap)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
                p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
                break
            p = p / s
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        p = p / s
        diff = 1.0 - float(p.sum())
        if diff > 1e-12:
            mask = p < cap
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                p[mask] += diff / cnt
                s = float(p.sum())
                if s > 0: p = p / s
            else:
                p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        elif diff < -1e-12:
            p = p / float(p.sum())
        return np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)
```
If cap*n=1 exactly (n=1000, cap=1), previous if cap>=1 returns. If n=1001, cap=0.999, cap*n=1000 >1. feasible. Good.

`normalize_a` should handle reshape. If `np.resize(a,n)` for wrong size may produce weird but okay.

```
    def normalize_a(x):
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            if a.size == 0:
                a = np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = np.resize(a, n)
        a = np.maximum(0.0, a)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = a / s
        if p.max() > p_cap + 1e-15:
            p = project_p(p, p_cap)
        # ensure sum1
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum <= 0 or not math.isfinite(psum):
            p = np.ones(n)/n
        else:
            p = p/psum
        return S * p
```
If p.max() slightly >cap but not much, skip; a max may be 1000+epsilon. Evaluator clips epsilon; negligible. Could call project if >cap. Use if >cap.

```
    def make_y(a):
       p = np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
       if p.size != n: ...
       p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=ones/n else p=p/s
       if p.max() > p_cap: p=project_p(p,p_cap)
       p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
       y = np.log(p)
       y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
       y -= float(y.mean())
       return np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
```

`y_to_a`:
```
    def y_to_a(y):
       y = np.asarray(y,dtype=float).ravel()
       if y.size != n: y=ones
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return full S/n
       y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
       ym = float(np.max(y))
       if not finite ym: return full
       ex = np.exp(y - ym)
       s = float(ex.sum())
       if s <= 1e-300 or not finite: p=ones/n else p=ex/s
       if p.max() > p_cap + 1e-15: p=project_p(p,p_cap)
       # If after project p max still >, okay
       return S * p
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    headroom = max(5.0, min(60.0, 0.10 * total))
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - headroom
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Load the supplied parent construction, if present.
    # We never paste/redefine its values.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        base = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    if base.size == 0:
        base = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(base)):
        base = np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if base.sum() <= 1e-300:
        base = np.ones(base.size, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(base.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return [1.0]

    # Very small budgets: return a safe scaled copy of the parent.
    if total < 45.0:
        a = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out = [float(x) for x in a]
        return out if out else [1.0]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fixed global scaling.  The objective is homogeneous, but using
    # sum(a) = n keeps coefficients O(1) and avoids evaluator clipping.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    S = float(n)
    S2 = S * S
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfftC = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    g_len = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    sizeG = 1 << (g_len - 1).bit_length()

    # Precompute slice metadata for direct active-set gradients.
    ks = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    lo_arr = np.maximum(0, ks - (n - 1))
    hi_arr = np.minimum(n - 1, ks) + 1
    start_arr = ks - (hi_arr - 1)
    stop_arr = ks - lo_arr + 1
    lo_l = lo_arr.tolist()
    hi_l = hi_arr.tolist()
    st_l = start_arr.tolist()
    sp_l = stop_arr.tolist()

    # Probability p = a / S must respect the evaluator's per-entry cap:
    # a_i <= 1000  <=>  p_i <= 1000/S when S > 1000.
    p_cap = 1000.0 / S if S > 1000.0 else 1.0
    p_cap = min(1.0, p_cap)
    if p_cap * n < 1.0 - 1e-12:
        p_cap = 1.0

    Y_LOW = -100.0
    Y_HIGH = 100.0

    def project_p(p, cap):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n if p.size == 0 else np.resize(p, n)
        p = np.maximum(0.0, p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        p = p / s
        cap = float(cap)
        if cap >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
            return p
        if cap * n < 1.0 - 1e-12:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

        for _ in range(12):
            mx = float(p.max())
            if mx <= cap + 1e-13:
                break
            p = np.minimum(p, cap)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
                p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
                break
            p = p / s

        s = float(p.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        p = p / s
        diff = 1.0 - float(p.sum())
        if diff > 1e-12:
            mask = p < cap
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                p[mask] += diff / cnt
                s = float(p.sum())
                if s > 0:
                    p = p / s
            else:
                p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        elif diff < -1e-12:
            p = p / float(p.sum())
        return np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)

    def normalize_a(x):
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            if a.size == 0:
                a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = np.resize(a, n)
        a = np.maximum(0.0, a)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = a / s
        if p.max() > p_cap + 1e-15:
            p = project_p(p, p_cap)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(ps):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = p / ps
        return S * p

    def make_y(a):
        p = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n if p.size == 0 else np.resize(p, n)
        p = np.maximum(0.0, p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = p / s
        if p.max() > p_cap + 1e-15:
            p = project_p(p, p_cap)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        y = np.log(p)
        y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
        y = y - float(y.mean())
        return np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)

    def y_to_a(y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if y.size != n:
            y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) if y.size == 0 else np.resize(y, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
        ym = float(y.max())
        if not math.isfinite(ym):
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        ex = np.exp(y - ym)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = ex / s
        if p.max() > p_cap + 1e-15:
            p = project_p(p, p_cap)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(ps):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            p = p / ps
        return S * p

    def fast_conv(a):
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfftC)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfftC)
        return out[:m]

    def score_from_value(x):
        return 2.0 * n * x / S2

    max_direct = 100

    def direct_grad(idx, wgt, a):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k, wt in zip(idx, wgt):
            wt = float(wt)
            if wt <= 1e-14:
                continue
            k = int(k)
            lo = lo_l[k]
            hi = hi_l[k]
            st = st_l[k]
            sp = sp_l[k]
            grad[lo:hi] += wt * a[st:sp][::-1]
        grad *= 2.0
        return grad

    def fft_grad(lam, a):
        rl = lam[::-1]
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, sizeG)
        fl = np.fft.rfft(rl, sizeG)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fl, sizeG)
        seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        return 2.0 * seg[::-1]

    def F_and_M(q, beta):
        M = float(q.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M):
            return 0.0, 0.0
        if beta <= 1e-8:
            return M, M
        z = beta * (q - M)
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        wsum = float(np.exp(z).sum())
        if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            return M, M
        return M + math.log(wsum) / beta, M

    def grad_F(q, a, beta):
        M = float(q.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M):
            return 0.0, 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if beta <= 1e-8:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            return M, M, direct_grad(idx, w, a)

        z = beta * (q - M)
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        wu = np.exp(z)
        wsum = float(wu.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            return M, M, direct_grad(idx, w, a)

        F = M + math.log(wsum) / beta
        threshold = 1e-8 * wsum
        idx = np.flatnonzero(wu > threshold)

        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            return F, M, direct_grad(idx, w, a)

        if idx.size > max_direct:
            lam = wu / wsum
            return F, M, fft_grad(lam, a)

        w = wu[idx] / wsum
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(sw):
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
            w = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / sw
        return F, M, direct_grad(idx, w, a)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Exact evaluation through the authoritative evaluator.  Use sparingly.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    best = {
        "m_score": math.inf,      # surrogate exact-max score using fast FFT
        "m_a": None,
        "m_y": None,
        "exact_score": math.inf,
        "exact_a": None,
        "last_exact": 0.0,
        "exact_count": 0,
    }

    def exact_score(a):
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            seq = [float(x) for x in a]
            v = evaluate_sequence(seq)
            v = float(v)
            return v if math.isfinite(v) else math.inf
        except Exception:
            return math.inf

    def consider_m(a, y=None):
        a = normalize_a(a)
        q = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(q.max())
        sc = score_from_value(M)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best["m_score"]:
            best["m_score"] = sc
            best["m_a"] = a.copy()
            best["m_y"] = make_y(a) if y is None else y
        return sc, a

    def exact_update(a):
        if not time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20:
            return math.inf
        if best["exact_count"] >= 30:
            return math.inf
        v = exact_score(a)
        best["exact_count"] += 1
        best["last_exact"] = time.monotonic()
        if math.isfinite(v) and v < best["exact_score"]:
            best["exact_score"] = v
            best["exact_a"] = normalize_a(a).copy()
        return v

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial constructions.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    base_n = normalize_a(base)
    sym_base = 0.5 * (base_n + base_n[::-1])

    candidates = [
        base_n,
        sym_base,
    ]

    # Compact one-power-edge and mixture seeds.  These can escape some
    # basins and also provide robust fallbacks if the parent is unavailable.
    try:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
        pw = x ** (-0.5)
        rp = pw[::-1]
        candidates.extend([
            pw,
            rp,
            pw + 0.25 * rp,
            pw + 0.5 * rp,
            pw + rp,
            np.sin(np.linspace(0.0, np.pi, n)) ** 2.0,
        ])
    except Exception:
        pass

    init_infos = []
    for c in candidates:
        try:
            a = normalize_a(c)
            q = fast_conv(a)
            M = float(q.max())
            sc = score_from_value(M)
            y = make_y(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                init_infos.append((sc, a, y))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not init_infos:
        base_n = normalize_a(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        init_infos = [(score_from_value(float(fast_conv(base_n).max())), base_n, make_y(base_n))]

    init_infos.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])

    # Establish surrogate best and a few exact anchors.
    for sc, a, y in init_infos:
        if sc < best["m_score"]:
            best["m_score"] = sc
            best["m_a"] = a.copy()
            best["m_y"] = y.copy()

    best_exact_seed_score = math.inf
    for sc, a, y in init_infos[:3]:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 90:
            break
        v = exact_update(a)
        if math.isfinite(v):
            best_exact_seed_score = min(best_exact_seed_score, v)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main optimizer: natural-gradient Adam on softmax logits, with a
    # smooth-max objective and backtracking line search.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def optimize(y0, end_time, lr0, beta0, beta1, max_restarts=2):
        start_time = time.monotonic()
        duration = max(1.0, end_time - start_time)

        y = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if y.size != n:
            y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64) if y.size == 0 else np.resize(y, n)
        y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
        y = y - float(y.mean())
        y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)

        a = y_to_a(y)
        q = fast_conv(a)
        beta = beta0
        F, M, grad = grad_F(q, a, beta)
        sF = score_from_value(F)
        sM = score_from_value(M)

        if not math.isfinite(sM):
            sM = score_from_value(float(fast_conv(normalize_a(base_n)).max()))
            a = normalize_a(base_n)
            q = fast_conv(a)
            y = make_y(a)

        local_best_score = sM
        local_best_a = a.copy()
        local_best_y = y.copy()

        if sM < best["m_score"]:
            best["m_score"] = sM
            best["m_a"] = a.copy()
            best["m_y"] = y.copy()

        b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
        m_adam = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v_adam = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        step = 0
        lr = max(1e-6, float(lr0))
        no_improve = 0
        fails = 0
        restarts = 0

        while time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
            now = time.monotonic()
            frac = min(1.0, (now - start_time) / (0.85 * duration))
            beta = beta0 + (beta1 - beta0) * frac

            F, M, grad = grad_F(q, a, beta)
            if not (math.isfinite(F) and math.isfinite(M)):
                break
            sF = score_from_value(F)
            sM = score_from_value(M)

            if math.isfinite(sM) and sM < local_best_score - 1e-12:
                local_best_score = sM
                local_best_a = a.copy()
                local_best_y = y.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if sM < best["m_score"]:
                    best["m_score"] = sM
                    best["m_a"] = a.copy()
                    best["m_y"] = y.copy()
                if (
                    best["exact_count"] < 20
                    and sM < best["exact_score"] - 1e-6
                    and time.monotonic() - best["last_exact"] > 25.0
                    and time.monotonic() < end_time - 40.0
                ):
                    exact_update(local_best_a)
                    continue

            p = a / S
            mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad))
            gy = S * p * (grad - mean_g)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)) or float(np.dot(gy, gy)) <= 1e-30:
                break

            step += 1
            m_adam = b1 * m_adam + (1.0 - b1) * gy
            v_adam = b2 * v_adam + (1.0 - b2) * (gy * gy)
            mb = m_adam / (1.0 - b1 ** step)
            vb = v_adam / (1.0 - b2 ** step)
            direction = -(mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + eps))

            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))
            if not math.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
                direction = -gy
                rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))
            if not math.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
                break
            direction = direction / rms

            alpha = lr
            accepted = False
            for _ in range(5):
                if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 1.0:
                    break
                y_trial = y + alpha * direction
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(y_trial)):
                    alpha *= 0.5
                    if alpha < 1e-10:
                        break
                    continue
                y_trial = np.clip(y_trial, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                a_trial = y_to_a(y_trial)
                q_trial = fast_conv(a_trial)
                F_trial, M_trial = F_and_M(q_trial, beta)
                if not (math.isfinite(F_trial) and math.isfinite(M_trial)):
                    alpha *= 0.5
                    if alpha < 1e-10:
                        break
                    continue
                sFt = score_from_value(F_trial)
                if sFt < sF - 1e-12:
                    y = y_trial
                    y = y - float(y.mean())
                    y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                    a = a_trial
                    q = q_trial
                    F = F_trial
                    M = M_trial
                    sF = sFt
                    sM = score_from_value(M_trial)
                    accepted = True
                    lr = min(0.5, max(1e-5, alpha * 1.1))
                    break
                alpha *= 0.5
                if alpha < 1e-8:
                    break

            if accepted:
                if math.isfinite(sM) and sM < local_best_score - 1e-12:
                    local_best_score = sM
                    local_best_a = a.copy()
                    local_best_y = y.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    if sM < best["m_score"]:
                        best["m_score"] = sM
                        best["m_a"] = a.copy()
                        best["m_y"] = y.copy()
                    if (
                        best["exact_count"] < 20
                        and sM < best["exact_score"] - 1e-6
                        and time.monotonic() - best["last_exact"] > 25.0
                        and time.monotonic() < end_time - 40.0
                    ):
                        exact_update(local_best_a)
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                fails = 0

                if no_improve > 1500 and restarts < max_restarts:
                    restarts += 1
                    scale = 0.12 / (restarts + 0.5)
                    y = local_best_y + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                    y = y - float(y.mean())
                    y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                    a = y_to_a(y)
                    q = fast_conv(a)
                    m_adam[:] = 0.0
                    v_adam[:] = 0.0
                    step = 0
                    lr = max(1e-3, lr0)
                    no_improve = 0
                    fails = 0
                    continue
            else:
                fails += 1
                no_improve += 1
                lr = max(1e-6, lr * 0.5)
                if fails > 40 and restarts < max_restarts:
                    restarts += 1
                    scale = 0.12 / (restarts + 0.5)
                    y = local_best_y + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                    y = y - float(y.mean())
                    y = np.clip(y, Y_LOW, Y_HIGH)
                    a = y_to_a(y)
                    q = fast_conv(a)
                    m_adam[:] = 0.0
                    v_adam[:] = 0.0
                    step = 0
                    lr = max(1e-3, lr0)
                    no_improve = 0
                    fails = 0
                    continue
                if fails > 80:
                    break

        if local_best_score < best["m_score"]:
            best["m_score"] = local_best_score
            best["m_a"] = local_best_a.copy()
            best["m_y"] = local_best_y.copy()
        return local_best_y, local_best_a, local_best_score

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Scheduled phases.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best["m_y"] is None:
        best["m_y"] = make_y(base_n)
        best["m_a"] = base_n.copy()

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, hard_deadline - now)

    if remaining > 80.0:
        # Main phase from the best seed.
        main_end = now + 0.55 * remaining
        optimize(best["m_y"], main_end, lr0=0.08, beta0=8.0, beta1=60.0, max_restarts=3)

    # Short exploratory phase from a different seed, if available.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, hard_deadline - now)
    if remaining > 60.0 and len(init_infos) >= 2:
        alt_sc, alt_a, alt_y = init_infos[1]
        if best["m_y"] is not None and alt_sc < best["m_score"] + 0.08:
            alt_end = now + 0.15 * remaining
            optimize(alt_y, alt_end, lr0=0.05, beta0=10.0, beta1=80.0, max_restarts=1)

    # Final verbose/sharp polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 30:
        polish_end = hard_deadline - 25.0
        if best["m_y"] is not None:
            optimize(best["m_y"], polish_end, lr0=0.02, beta0=40.0, beta1=220.0, max_restarts=1)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final validation with the authoritative exact evaluator.
    # Prefer exact best; also test a few simple projections/symmetries.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    final_candidates = []

    def add_final(a):
        if a is None:
            return
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                a = normalize_a(a)
                if len(final_candidates) < 8:
                    final_candidates.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_final(best["exact_a"])
    add_final(best["m_a"])
    if best["m_a"] is not None:
        add_final(0.5 * (best["m_a"] + best["m_a"][::-1]))
    add_final(base_n)
    add_final(sym_base)

    final_a = None
    final_score = math.inf
    for a in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 10:
            break
        v = exact_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(v) and v < final_score:
            final_score = v
            final_a = a

    if final_a is None:
        final_a = best["m_a"] if best["m_a"] is not None else base_n

    final_a = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if final_a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(final_a)):
        final_a = base_n

    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        final_a = base_n
        final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(final_a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            final_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    if not out or any((not math.isfinite(x)) for x in out):
        out = [1.0] * n

    return out
```
